Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous decision-makers in strategic settings, yet we have limited tools for understanding their high-level behavioral traits. We use activation steering methods in game-theoretic settings, constructing persona vectors for altruism, forgiveness, and expectations of others by contrastive activation addition. Evaluating on canonical games, we find that activation steering systematically shifts both quantitative strategic choices and natural-language justifications. However, we also observe that rhetoric and strategy can diverge under steering. In addition, vectors for self-behavior and expectations of others are partially distinct. Our results suggest that persona vectors offer a promising mechanistic handle on high-level traits in strategic environments.

</details>


### [150] [The Myhill-Nerode Theorem for Bounded Interaction: Canonical Abstractions via Agent-Bounded Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21399)
*Anthony T. Nixon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于有限状态控制器的POMDP环境商空间构造方法，通过Wasserstein伪度量合并不可区分的观测历史，建立了有界交互的Myhill-Nerode定理类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何容量有限的观测者都会在其环境中诱导出规范商空间：有界智能体无法区分的两种情况对该智能体而言是相同的。需要为有限POMDP形式化这一概念，构建最小、规范且唯一的商空间结构。

Method: 使用固定探针族（有限状态控制器）在观测历史上诱导闭环Wasserstein伪度量，构造探针精确商空间合并控制器族无法区分的观测历史。对于时钟感知探针，该商空间对仅依赖观测和行动的目标是决策充分的；对于潜在状态奖励，使用观测-Lipschitz近似边界。

Result: 建立了有界交互的Myhill-Nerode定理类比：商空间是规范、最小且唯一的。在Tiger和GridWorld上验证了定理级声明。通过Tiger、GridWorld和RockSample的案例研究探索了近似行为和运行时特性。

Conclusion: 提出的时钟感知商空间为有限POMDP中的有界交互提供了理论基础，通过探针族诱导的度量结构实现了观测历史的规范划分，为部分可观测环境中的决策充分性提供了形式化框架。

Abstract: Any capacity-limited observer induces a canonical quotient on its environment: two situations that no bounded agent can distinguish are, for that agent, the same. We formalise this for finite POMDPs. A fixed probe family of finite-state controllers induces a closed-loop Wasserstein pseudometric on observation histories and a probe-exact quotient merging histories that no controller in the family can distinguish. The quotient is canonical, minimal, and unique-a bounded-interaction analogue of the Myhill-Nerode theorem. For clock-aware probes, it is exactly decision-sufficient for objectives that depend only on the agent's observations and actions; for latent-state rewards, we use an observation-Lipschitz approximation bound. The main theorem object is the clock-aware quotient; scalable deterministic-stationary experiments study a tractable coarsening with gap measured on small exact cases and explored empirically at larger scale. We validate theorem-level claims on Tiger and GridWorld. We also report operational case studies on Tiger, GridWorld, and RockSample as exploratory diagnostics of approximation behavior and runtime, not as theorem-facing evidence when no exact cross-family certificate is available; heavier stress tests are archived in the appendix and artifact package.

</details>


### [151] [Is the future of AI green? What can innovation diffusion models say about generative AI's environmental impact?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21419)
*Robert Viseur,Nicolas Jullien*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用A-U创新扩散模型分析生成式AI生态系统，预测其环境影响演变，认为虽然GAI永远不会是绿色的，但其影响可能不如某些声称的那么严重，但取决于主导商业模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对生成式AI环境影响的预测往往忽略了创新扩散过程中产品演化和性能优化（主要出于经济原因）也能减少环境影响的事实，需要更全面的分析框架。

Method: 采用经典的A-U（Abernathy-Utterback）创新扩散模型来分析生成式AI生态系统，预测行业结构及其环境影响的演变路径。

Result: 分析表明，虽然生成式AI永远不会是绿色的，但其环境影响可能不像某些预测那样严重，但这一结论高度依赖于最终主导的商业模式选择。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的环境影响需要通过创新扩散和产品演化的视角来理解，经济驱动的优化可能缓解其环境影响，但商业模式的选择至关重要。

Abstract: The rise of generative artificial intelligence (GAI) has led to alarming predictions about its environmental impact. However, these predictions often overlook the fact that the diffusion of innovation is accompanied by the evolution of products and the optimization of their performance, primarily for economic reasons. This can also reduce their environmental impact. By analyzing the GAI ecosystem using the classic A-U innovation diffusion model, we can forecast this industry's structure and how its environmental impact will evolve. While GAI will never be green, its impact may not be as problematic as is sometimes claimed. However, this depends on which business model becomes dominant.

</details>


### [152] [Safety as Computation: Certified Answer Reuse via Capability Closure in Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21448)
*Cosimo Spera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务导向对话系统新范式：将安全认证作为答案重用的计算原语，通过认证答案存储和预答案块实现亚毫秒级查询响应，消除冗余检索和生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对话系统将每个对话轮次独立处理，即使答案已可从先前状态推导，仍通过检索或生成重新计算答案，导致效率低下

Method: 提出认证答案存储(CAS)和预答案块(PAB)方法：在认证轮次中，系统物化所有可推导的后续答案及最小来源见证；后续查询通过形式化包含检查实现亚毫秒级响应

Result: 在基于能力的系统中，安全认证步骤计算固定点闭包cl(At)，已包含当前配置下所有可达答案；后续查询无需冗余检索和生成

Conclusion: 安全认证可作为答案重用的有效计算原语，通过认证答案存储和预答案块显著提升对话系统效率，实现亚毫秒级查询响应

Abstract: We introduce a new paradigm for task-oriented dialogue systems: safety certification as a computational primitive for answer reuse. Current systems treat each turn independently, recomputing answers via retrieval or generation even when they are already derivable from prior state. We show that in capability-based systems, the safety certification step computes a fixed-point closure cl(At) that already contains every answer reachable from the current configuration. We operationalize this insight with a Certified Answer Store (CAS) augmented by Pre-Answer Blocks (PAB): at each certified turn, the system materializes all derivable follow-up answers together with minimal provenance witnesses. Subsequent queries are answered in sub-millisecond time via formal containment checks, eliminating redundant retrieval and generation.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Correlation: Refutation-Validated Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis for Explainable Energy Market Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21473)
*Wihan van der Heever,Keane Ong,Ranjan Satapathy,Erik Cambria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个反驳验证框架用于金融市场基于方面的情感分析，解决相关性研究无法区分真实关联与虚假关联的局限性，在能源板块股票上测试方面级情感信号与股票收益的稳健关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相关性研究在金融市场情感分析中存在局限性，无法区分真实关联与虚假关联，需要建立更稳健的统计验证框架来确保分析结果的可靠性。

Method: 结合净比率评分与z标准化、使用Newey West HAC误差的OLS回归，以及包括安慰剂检验、随机共同原因检验、子集稳定性检验和自助法在内的反驳验证测试。

Result: 在六个能源股票中，只有少数关联通过了所有验证检查，可再生能源股票显示出方面和时域特定的响应，样本规模有限（六只股票，一个季度）限制了普遍性。

Conclusion: 虽然未建立因果关系，但该框架提供了统计上稳健、方向可解释的信号，主要作为方法论的概念验证，强调了反驳验证在金融情感分析中的重要性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a refutation-validated framework for aspect-based sentiment analysis in financial markets, addressing the limitations of correlational studies that cannot distinguish genuine associations from spurious ones. Using X data for the energy sector, we test whether aspect-level sentiment signals show robust, refutation-validated relationships with equity returns. Our pipeline combines net-ratio scoring with z-normalization, OLS with Newey West HAC errors, and refutation tests including placebo, random common cause, subset stability, and bootstrap. Across six energy tickers, only a few associations survive all checks, while renewables show aspect and horizon specific responses. While not establishing causality, the framework provides statistically robust, directionally interpretable signals, with limited sample size (six stocks, one quarter) constraining generalizability and framing this work as a methodological proof of concept.

</details>


### [154] [Unified-MAS: Universally Generating Domain-Specific Nodes for Empowering Automatic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21475)
*Hehai Lin,Yu Yan,Zixuan Wang,Bo Xu,Sudong Wang,Weiquan Huang,Ruochen Zhao,Minzhi Li,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Unified-MAS通过离线节点合成将细粒度节点实现与拓扑编排解耦，在知识密集型领域提升自动多智能体系统性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动多智能体系统框架在知识密集型领域存在瓶颈：要么依赖通用节点库缺乏专业知识，要么在线生成节点导致架构耦合，同时受限于LLM内部知识限制

Method: 提出两阶段方法：1) 基于搜索的节点生成，检索外部开放世界知识合成专业节点蓝图；2) 基于奖励的节点优化，使用困惑度引导的奖励迭代增强瓶颈节点内部逻辑

Result: 在四个专业领域实验中，将Unified-MAS集成到四个自动多智能体基线中，实现了更好的性能-成本权衡，性能提升高达14.2%同时显著降低成本

Conclusion: Unified-MAS通过解耦节点实现与拓扑编排，有效解决了知识密集型领域中自动多智能体系统的架构耦合问题，在不同设计器LLM上表现出鲁棒性，在数学推理等传统任务上也有效

Abstract: Automatic Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) generation has emerged as a promising paradigm for solving complex reasoning tasks. However, existing frameworks are fundamentally bottlenecked when applied to knowledge-intensive domains (e.g., healthcare and law). They either rely on a static library of general nodes like Chain-of-Thought, which lack specialized expertise, or attempt to generate nodes on the fly. In the latter case, the orchestrator is not only bound by its internal knowledge limits but must also simultaneously generate domain-specific logic and optimize high-level topology, leading to a severe architectural coupling that degrades overall system efficacy. To bridge this gap, we propose Unified-MAS that decouples granular node implementation from topological orchestration via offline node synthesis. Unified-MAS operates in two stages: (1) Search-Based Node Generation retrieves external open-world knowledge to synthesize specialized node blueprints, overcoming the internal knowledge limits of LLMs; and (2) Reward-Based Node Optimization utilizes a perplexity-guided reward to iteratively enhance the internal logic of bottleneck nodes. Extensive experiments across four specialized domains demonstrate that integrating Unified-MAS into four Automatic-MAS baselines yields a better performance-cost trade-off, achieving up to a 14.2% gain while significantly reducing costs. Further analysis reveals its robustness across different designer LLMs and its effectiveness on conventional tasks such as mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [155] [Counterfactual Credit Policy Optimization for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21563)
*Zhongyi Li,Wan Tian,Yikun Ban,Jinju Chen,Huiming Zhang,Yang Liu,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CCPO是一个用于协作式多智能体LLM训练的反事实信用分配框架，通过估计每个智能体的边际贡献来解决共享全局奖励导致的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式多智能体LLM系统在复杂推理任务中面临信用分配挑战：共享的全局奖励掩盖了个体贡献，导致更新方差增大和"搭便车"问题，限制了强化学习效果。

Method: 提出反事实信用策略优化(CCPO)框架：1)构建动态反事实基线，模拟移除智能体贡献后的结果；2)生成角色敏感的优势函数用于策略优化；3)引入全局历史感知的归一化方案，利用全局rollout统计校准优势值。

Result: 在顺序思考-推理二人组和多智能体投票两种协作拓扑上，CCPO在数学和逻辑推理基准测试中有效缓解了搭便车问题，优于现有多智能体RL基线，实现了更细粒度和有效的信用分配。

Conclusion: CCPO通过反事实信用分配机制显著改善了协作式多智能体LLM训练的效率和效果，为解决多智能体系统中的信用分配问题提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Collaborative multi-agent large language models (LLMs) can solve complex reasoning tasks by decomposing roles and aggregating diverse hypotheses. Yet, reinforcement learning (RL) for such systems is often undermined by credit assignment: a shared global reward obscures individual contributions, inflating update variance and encouraging free-riding. We introduce Counterfactual Credit Policy Optimization (CCPO), a framework that assigns agent-specific learning signals by estimating each agent's marginal contribution through counterfactual trajectories. CCPO builds dynamic counterfactual baselines that simulate outcomes with an agent's contribution removed, yielding role-sensitive advantages for policy optimization. To further improve stability under heterogeneous tasks and data distributions, we propose a global-history-aware normalization scheme that calibrates advantages using global rollout statistics. We evaluate CCPO on two collaboration topologies: a sequential Think--Reason dyad and multi-agent voting. Across mathematical and logical reasoning benchmarks, CCPO mitigates free-riding and outperforms strong multi-agent RL baselines, yielding finer-grained and more effective credit assignment for collaborative LLM training. Our code is available at https://github.com/bhai114/ccpo.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Robust Estimator for Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21574)
*Zhongyi Li,Wan Tian,Jingyu Chen,Kangyao Huang,Huiming Zhang,Hui Yang,Tao Ren,Jinyang Jiang,Yijie Peng,Yikun Ban,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DACR+ARE框架解决多智能体协作推理中的角色模糊和奖励噪声问题，在数学推理和具身智能任务中表现优于基线


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作增强大语言模型推理能力，但存在交互层面的角色模糊问题（生成、批评、修订界限不清），导致信用分配困难；同时策略优化易受重尾和噪声奖励影响，使优势估计偏差并引发训练不稳定甚至发散

Method: 提出鲁棒多智能体强化学习框架：1) DACR（双智能体答案-批评-重写）将推理分解为结构化三阶段流水线，明确归属各智能体对伙伴性能的边际贡献；2) ARE（自适应鲁棒估计器）在多智能体策略优化中提供批量经验均值的鲁棒估计

Result: 在数学推理和具身智能基准测试中，即使在噪声奖励下，该方法在齐次和非齐次设置中均一致优于基线，表现出对奖励噪声更强的鲁棒性和更稳定的训练动态，有效防止噪声奖励信号导致的优化失败

Conclusion: DACR+ARE框架成功解决了多智能体协作推理中的角色模糊和奖励噪声问题，实现了更鲁棒和稳定的训练，为多智能体强化学习在复杂推理任务中的应用提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models, yet it suffers from interaction-level ambiguity that blurs generation, critique, and revision, making credit assignment across agents difficult. Moreover, policy optimization in this setting is vulnerable to heavy-tailed and noisy rewards, which can bias advantage estimation and trigger unstable or even divergent training. To address both issues, we propose a robust multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for collaborative reasoning, consisting of two components: Dual-Agent Answer-Critique-Rewrite (DACR) and an Adaptive Robust Estimator (ARE). DACR decomposes reasoning into a structured three-stage pipeline: answer, critique, and rewrite, while enabling explicit attribution of each agent's marginal contribution to its partner's performance. ARE provides robust estimation of batch experience means during multi-agent policy optimization. Across mathematical reasoning and embodied intelligence benchmarks, even under noisy rewards, our method consistently outperforms the baseline in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings. These results indicate stronger robustness to reward noise and more stable training dynamics, effectively preventing optimization failures caused by noisy reward signals.

</details>


### [157] [Mind over Space: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Mentally Navigate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21577)
*Qihui Zhu,Shouwei Ruan,Xiao Yang,Hao Jiang,Yao Huang,Shiji Zhao,Hanwei Fan,Hang Su,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Video2Mental基准测试评估MLLMs的心理导航能力，发现现有模型在长时空尺度空间推理方面存在不足，并开发了NavMind模型通过显式认知地图显著提升心理导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs在具身智能体中的应用主要局限于基于即时观察的反应式规划，缺乏在广泛时空尺度上的空间推理能力。认知科学研究表明，生物智能通过"心理导航"——从经验构建空间表征并进行行动前的心理路径模拟——而蓬勃发展。需要弥合AI与BI在这一关键能力上的差距。

Method: 1) 提出Video2Mental基准测试，要求从长时第一人称视角视频构建分层认知地图，并生成基于地标的逐步路径规划，通过模拟器物理交互验证规划准确性；2) 开发NavMind推理模型，使用显式、细粒度的认知地图作为可学习的中间表征来内化心理导航；3) 采用难度分层的渐进式监督微调范式，弥合原始感知与结构化规划之间的差距。

Result: 基准测试结果显示：1) 心理导航能力不会从标准预训练中自然涌现；2) 前沿MLLMs在零样本结构化空间表征方面表现困难；3) 规划准确性在扩展时间范围内急剧衰减。NavMind模型在心理导航能力方面表现优异，显著超越了前沿商业和空间MLLMs。

Conclusion: 论文通过Video2Mental基准测试揭示了当前MLLMs在心理导航能力上的局限性，并提出NavMind模型通过显式认知地图和渐进式微调有效提升了空间推理能力，为弥合AI与生物智能在空间认知方面的差距提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of MLLMs in embodied agents, their capabilities remain largely confined to reactive planning from immediate observations, consistently failing in spatial reasoning across extensive spatiotemporal scales. Cognitive science reveals that Biological Intelligence (BI) thrives on "mental navigation": the strategic construction of spatial representations from experience and the subsequent mental simulation of paths prior to action. To bridge the gap between AI and BI, we introduce Video2Mental, a pioneering benchmark for evaluating the mental navigation capabilities of MLLMs. The task requires constructing hierarchical cognitive maps from long egocentric videos and generating landmark-based path plans step by step, with planning accuracy verified through simulator-based physical interaction. Our benchmarking results reveal that mental navigation capability does not naturally emerge from standard pre-training. Frontier MLLMs struggle profoundly with zero-shot structured spatial representation, and their planning accuracy decays precipitously over extended horizons. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{NavMind}, a reasoning model that internalizes mental navigation using explicit, fine-grained cognitive maps as learnable intermediate representations. Through a difficulty-stratified progressive supervised fine-tuning paradigm, NavMind effectively bridges the gap between raw perception and structured planning. Experiments demonstrate that NavMind achieves superior mental navigation capabilities, significantly outperforming frontier commercial and spatial MLLMs.

</details>


### [158] [A Multidisciplinary AI Board for Multimodal Dementia Characterization and Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21597)
*Sheng Liu,Long Chen,Zeyun Zhao,Qinglin Gou,Qingyue Wei,Arjun Masurkar,Kevin M. Spiegler,Philip Kuball,Stefania C. Bray,Megan Bernath,Deanna R. Willis,Jiang Bian,Lei Xing,Eric Topol,Kyunghyun Cho,Yu Huang,Ruogu Fang,Narges Razavian,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cerebra是一个交互式多智能体AI系统，通过协调专门处理电子病历、临床笔记和医学影像的智能体，为临床决策提供支持，在痴呆风险预测、诊断和生存预测等任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代临床实践需要处理异构、动态且不完整的患者数据，但现有多模态基础模型多为静态、不透明且与真实临床工作流程不匹配。需要开发能够整合多种数据源、支持交互式临床决策的系统。

Method: 开发了Cerebra系统，这是一个交互式多智能体AI团队，协调专门处理电子病历、临床笔记和医学影像分析的智能体。系统将分析结果合成为面向临床医生的仪表板，结合可视化分析和对话界面，支持隐私保护部署，并能处理不完整模态数据。

Result: 在包含300万患者的多机构数据集上评估，Cerebra在痴呆风险预测中AUROC达到0.80（单模态模型0.74，语言模型基线0.68），痴呆诊断AUROC 0.86，生存预测C-index 0.81。在医师读者研究中，将专家在痴呆风险估计中的准确率提高了17.5个百分点。

Conclusion: Cerebra展示了在临床护理中提供可解释、鲁棒决策支持的潜力，通过多智能体协调和交互式界面，显著提升了临床决策的准确性和实用性。

Abstract: Modern clinical practice increasingly depends on reasoning over heterogeneous, evolving, and incomplete patient data. Although recent advances in multimodal foundation models have improved performance on various clinical tasks, most existing models remain static, opaque, and poorly aligned with real-world clinical workflows. We present Cerebra, an interactive multi-agent AI team that coordinates specialized agents for EHR, clinical notes, and medical imaging analysis. These outputs are synthesized into a clinician-facing dashboard that combines visual analytics with a conversational interface, enabling clinicians to interrogate predictions and contextualize risk at the point of care. Cerebra supports privacy-preserving deployment by operating on structured representations and remains robust when modalities are incomplete. We evaluated Cerebra using a massive multi-institutional dataset spanning 3 million patients from four independent healthcare systems. Cerebra consistently outperformed both state-of-the-art single-modality models and large multimodal language model baselines. In dementia risk prediction, it achieved AUROCs up to 0.80, compared with 0.74 for the strongest single-modality model and 0.68 for language model baselines. For dementia diagnosis, it achieved an AUROC of 0.86, and for survival prediction, a C-index of 0.81. In a reader study with experienced physicians, Cerebra significantly improved expert performance, increasing accuracy by 17.5 percentage points in prospective dementia risk estimation. These results demonstrate Cerebra's potential for interpretable, robust decision support in clinical care.

</details>


### [159] [INTRYGUE: Induction-Aware Entropy Gating for Reliable RAG Uncertainty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21607)
*Alexandra Bazarova,Andrei Volodichev,Daria Kotova,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了基于熵的不确定性量化方法在RAG中因机制性悖论而失效，提出了基于归纳头激活模式的熵门控方法INTRYGUE，在多个基准测试中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成显著提高了LLM的事实可靠性，但无法完全消除幻觉，因此需要鲁棒的不确定性量化。研究发现标准基于熵的UQ方法在RAG设置中经常失败，原因是存在机制性悖论：归纳头在促进接地响应时会触发"熵神经元"，导致预测熵膨胀，使模型对准确输出发出虚假不确定性信号。

Method: 提出了INTRYGUE方法，这是一种基于机制的方法，通过归纳头的激活模式对预测熵进行门控。该方法结合了预测不确定性和可解释的内部上下文利用信号，通过检测归纳头的激活模式来调整不确定性估计。

Result: 在四个RAG基准测试和六个开源LLM（4B到13B参数）上评估，INTRYGUE始终匹配或优于广泛的UQ基线方法。实验表明该方法能有效解决标准熵方法在RAG中的失效问题。

Conclusion: RAG中的幻觉检测受益于将预测不确定性与可解释的内部上下文利用信号相结合。INTRYGUE通过机制性方法解决了熵膨胀问题，为RAG系统提供了更可靠的不确定性量化。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) significantly improves the factual reliability of LLMs, it does not eliminate hallucinations, so robust uncertainty quantification (UQ) remains essential. In this paper, we reveal that standard entropy-based UQ methods often fail in RAG settings due to a mechanistic paradox. An internal "tug-of-war" inherent to context utilization appears: while induction heads promote grounded responses by copying the correct answer, they collaterally trigger the previously established "entropy neurons". This interaction inflates predictive entropy, causing the model to signal false uncertainty on accurate outputs. To address this, we propose INTRYGUE (Induction-Aware Entropy Gating for Uncertainty Estimation), a mechanistically grounded method that gates predictive entropy based on the activation patterns of induction heads. Evaluated across four RAG benchmarks and six open-source LLMs (4B to 13B parameters), INTRYGUE consistently matches or outperforms a wide range of UQ baselines. Our findings demonstrate that hallucination detection in RAG benefits from combining predictive uncertainty with interpretable, internal signals of context utilization.

</details>


### [160] [Silicon Bureaucracy and AI Test-Oriented Education: Contamination Sensitivity and Score Confidence in LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21636)
*Yiliang Song,Hongjun An,Jiangan Chen,Xuanchen Yan,Huan Song,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"硅官僚主义"和"AI应试教育"概念，批评当前LLM基准测试假设分数直接反映泛化能力，但实际上可能混淆应试能力和真实能力，建议通过污染敏感性审计框架评估基准分数可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估过度依赖基准测试分数，但存在两个问题：1) 基准分数可能无法真实反映模型的泛化能力；2) 训练数据污染和语义泄露难以避免，导致分数可能反映的是对特定测试形式的熟悉度而非真实能力。

Method: 提出污染敏感性审计框架，采用路由器-工作者设置，比较清洁控制条件与噪声条件。在噪声条件下，对基准问题进行系统性的删除、重写和扰动处理，然后传递给下游模型。如果基准真正清洁，噪声条件不应持续优于清洁控制基线。

Result: 在多个模型中发现广泛但异质的超基线增益：噪声条件下模型表现优于清洁控制条件，表明基准相关线索可能被重新组装并激活污染相关记忆。这意味着相似的基准分数可能具有显著不同的可信度水平。

Conclusion: 不应完全拒绝基准测试，但基准评估应补充污染敏感性和分数可信度的显式审计。基准分数需要更细致的解释，应考虑模型对基准特定线索的依赖程度。

Abstract: Public benchmarks increasingly govern how large language models (LLMs) are ranked, selected, and deployed. We frame this benchmark-centered regime as Silicon Bureaucracy and AI Test-Oriented Education, and argue that it rests on a fragile assumption: that benchmark scores directly reflect genuine generalization. In practice, however, such scores may conflate exam-oriented competence with principled capability, especially when contamination and semantic leakage are difficult to exclude from modern training pipelines. We therefore propose an audit framework for analyzing contamination sensitivity and score confidence in LLM benchmarks. Using a router-worker setup, we compare a clean-control condition with noisy conditions in which benchmark problems are systematically deleted, rewritten, and perturbed before being passed downstream. For a genuinely clean benchmark, noisy conditions should not consistently outperform the clean-control baseline. Yet across multiple models, we find widespread but heterogeneous above-baseline gains under noisy conditions, indicating that benchmark-related cues may be reassembled and can reactivate contamination-related memory. These results suggest that similar benchmark scores may carry substantially different levels of confidence. Rather than rejecting benchmarks altogether, we argue that benchmark-based evaluation should be supplemented with explicit audits of contamination sensitivity and score confidence.

</details>


### [161] [Mirage The Illusion of Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21687)
*Mohammad Asadi,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Fang Cao,Tahoura Nedaee,Kamyar Fardi,Fei-Fei Li,Ehsan Adeli,Euan Ashley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态AI系统存在"海市蜃楼推理"现象：前沿模型会为未提供的图像生成详细描述和推理痕迹，在无图像输入时仍能在多模态基准测试中获得高分，暴露了视觉-语言模型推理和评估的根本性漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态AI系统在现实任务中表现出色，但其视觉-语言推理机制仍未被充分理解。研究旨在揭示这些系统如何处理和整合视觉信息，特别是当它们在没有实际图像输入时仍能表现良好的现象。

Method: 通过三个关键实验：1) 测试模型为未提供图像生成描述和推理的能力（海市蜃楼推理）；2) 评估模型在无图像输入时在多模态基准测试中的表现；3) 比较显式猜测与隐式假设图像存在时的性能差异。最后提出B-Clean作为解决方案。

Result: 1) 模型会为从未提供的图像生成详细描述和临床发现；2) 无图像输入时模型在通用和医学多模态基准测试中获得惊人高分，甚至在某胸部X光问答基准中排名第一；3) 显式要求猜测而非假设图像存在时，性能显著下降，表明存在不同的响应机制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了视觉-语言模型推理和评估的根本性漏洞，特别是在医学等高风险领域。需要开发消除文本线索的私有基准测试，确保公平的视觉基础评估。B-Clean被提出作为解决这一问题的原则性方案。

Abstract: Multimodal AI systems have achieved remarkable performance across a broad range of real-world tasks, yet the mechanisms underlying visual-language reasoning remain surprisingly poorly understood. We report three findings that challenge prevailing assumptions about how these systems process and integrate visual information. First, Frontier models readily generate detailed image descriptions and elaborate reasoning traces, including pathology-biased clinical findings, for images never provided; we term this phenomenon mirage reasoning. Second, without any image input, models also attain strikingly high scores across general and medical multimodal benchmarks, bringing into question their utility and design. In the most extreme case, our model achieved the top rank on a standard chest X-ray question-answering benchmark without access to any images. Third, when models were explicitly instructed to guess answers without image access, rather than being implicitly prompted to assume images were present, performance declined markedly. Explicit guessing appears to engage a more conservative response regime, in contrast to the mirage regime in which models behave as though images have been provided. These findings expose fundamental vulnerabilities in how visual-language models reason and are evaluated, pointing to an urgent need for private benchmarks that eliminate textual cues enabling non-visual inference, particularly in medical contexts where miscalibrated AI carries the greatest consequence. We introduce B-Clean as a principled solution for fair, vision-grounded evaluation of multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [162] [Deterministic Hallucination Detection in Medical VQA via Confidence-Evidence Bayesian Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21693)
*Mohammad Asadi,Tahoura Nedaee,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CEBaG方法，一种无需随机采样、外部模型或任务特定超参数的确定性幻觉检测方法，用于医疗多模态大语言模型，通过结合令牌级预测方差和证据强度来检测幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态大语言模型在视觉问答中存在幻觉问题，现有检测方法（如SE和VASE）需要大量随机生成样本和外部自然语言推理模型，计算成本高且难以实际部署。

Method: 提出置信度-证据贝叶斯增益（CEBaG）方法，结合两个互补信号：1）令牌级预测方差，捕捉响应令牌间不一致的置信度；2）证据强度，衡量图像相对于纯文本推理对每个令牌预测的偏移程度。

Result: 在四个医疗MLLM和三个VQA基准测试（16个实验设置）中，CEBaG在13个设置中达到最高AUC，平均比VASE提高8个AUC点，同时完全确定性和自包含。

Conclusion: CEBaG通过分析模型自身对数概率中的特征，提供了一种高效、自包含的幻觉检测方法，显著优于现有方法，有望在临床环境中实际部署。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential for medical Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet they remain prone to hallucinations, defined as generating responses that contradict the input image, posing serious risks in clinical settings. Current hallucination detection methods, such as Semantic Entropy (SE) and Vision-Amplified Semantic Entropy (VASE), require 10 to 20 stochastic generations per sample together with an external natural language inference model for semantic clustering, making them computationally expensive and difficult to deploy in practice. We observe that hallucinated responses exhibit a distinctive signature directly in the model's own log-probabilities: inconsistent token-level confidence and weak sensitivity to visual evidence. Based on this observation, we propose Confidence-Evidence Bayesian Gain (CEBaG), a deterministic hallucination detection method that requires no stochastic sampling, no external models, and no task-specific hyperparameters. CEBaG combines two complementary signals: token-level predictive variance, which captures inconsistent confidence across response tokens, and evidence magnitude, which measures how much the image shifts per-token predictions relative to text-only inference. Evaluated across four medical MLLMs and three VQA benchmarks (16 experimental settings), CEBaG achieves the highest AUC in 13 of 16 settings and improves over VASE by 8 AUC points on average, while being fully deterministic and self-contained. The code will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [163] [MIND: Multi-agent inference for negotiation dialogue in travel planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21696)
*Hunmin Do,Taejun Yoon,Kiyong Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIND框架通过心智理论增强多智能体辩论，在旅行规划等复杂利益协调中实现更有效的共识构建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论研究在协调复杂利益相关者（如旅行规划中的异质偏好）方面的效果尚未充分探索，需要更现实的共识构建框架

Method: 提出MIND框架，基于心智理论引入战略评估阶段，通过语言细微差别推断对手意愿度，准确率达90.2%

Result: MIND优于传统MAD框架：高意愿命中率提升20.5%，辩论命中率提升30.7%；LLM评估显示理性度68.8%、流畅度72.4%，总体胜率68.3%

Conclusion: MIND能有效建模人类谈判动态，生成有说服力的共识，为复杂利益协调提供了有效的多智能体推理框架

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) research has advanced, its efficacy in coordinating complex stakeholder interests such as travel planning remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose MIND (Multi-agent Inference for Negotiation Dialogue), a framework designed to simulate realistic consensus-building among travelers with heterogeneous preferences. Grounded in the Theory of Mind (ToM), MIND introduces a Strategic Appraisal phase that infers opponent willingness (w) from linguistic nuances with 90.2% accuracy. Experimental results demonstrate that MIND outperforms traditional MAD frameworks, achieving a 20.5% improvement in High-w Hit and a 30.7% increase in Debate Hit-Rate, effectively prioritizing high-stakes constraints. Furthermore, qualitative evaluations via LLM-as-a-Judge confirm that MIND surpasses baselines in Rationality (68.8%) and Fluency (72.4%), securing an overall win rate of 68.3%. These findings validate that MIND effectively models human negotiation dynamics to derive persuasive consensus.

</details>


### [164] [Compensating Visual Insufficiency with Stratified Language Guidance for Long-Tail Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21708)
*Xi Wang,Xu Yang,Donghao Sun,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种利用语言知识解决长尾类增量学习问题的方法，通过分层语言树和自适应语言指导来缓解数据不平衡和灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾类增量学习面临双重挑战：尾部类别样本稀缺不仅影响其学习效果，还会在持续演化的不平衡数据分布下加剧灾难性遗忘。现有方法难以同时应对数据不平衡和持续学习问题。

Method: 1) 分析LT CIL数据分布，指导大语言模型生成分层语言树，从粗到细粒度组织语义信息；2) 提出分层自适应语言指导，使用可学习权重融合多尺度语义表示，动态调整尾部类别的监督信号；3) 提出分层对齐语言指导，利用语言树的结构稳定性约束优化，增强语义视觉对齐。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，该方法实现了最先进的性能，有效缓解了数据不平衡和灾难性遗忘问题。

Conclusion: 通过利用语言知识的信息性和可扩展性，提出的分层语言指导方法能够有效解决长尾类增量学习中的核心挑战，为不平衡持续学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-tail class incremental learning (LT CIL) remains highly challenging because the scarcity of samples in tail classes not only hampers their learning but also exacerbates catastrophic forgetting under continuously evolving and imbalanced data distributions. To tackle these issues, we exploit the informativeness and scalability of language knowledge. Specifically, we analyze the LT CIL data distribution to guide large language models (LLMs) in generating a stratified language tree that hierarchically organizes semantic information from coarse to fine grained granularity. Building upon this structure, we introduce stratified adaptive language guidance, which leverages learnable weights to merge multi-scale semantic representations, thereby enabling dynamic supervisory adjustment for tail classes and alleviating the impact of data imbalance. Furthermore, we introduce stratified alignment language guidance, which exploits the structural stability of the language tree to constrain optimization and reinforce semantic visual alignment, thereby alleviating catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state of the art performance.

</details>


### [165] [CurvZO: Adaptive Curvature-Guided Sparse Zeroth-Order Optimization for Efficient LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21725)
*Shuo Wang,Ziyu Chen,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CurvZO是一种自适应曲率引导的稀疏零阶优化方法，通过在线跟踪曲率信号来构建参数采样分布，动态调整扰动预算，在保持内存效率的同时提高LLM微调性能和训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反向传播微调LLMs内存开销大，限制了在资源受限硬件上的可扩展性。零阶优化虽然内存效率高，但梯度估计方差大导致收敛慢或不稳定。稀疏零阶更新需要选择信息丰富的参数，但在零阶优化中由于每个查询只产生标量反馈，参数选择具有挑战性。

Method: 提出自适应曲率引导稀疏零阶优化(CurvZO)：1) 从标量零阶反馈中在线跟踪曲率信号；2) 利用这些信号构建参数级采样分布来选择每次更新的坐标，降低稀疏零阶梯度估计器的方差；3) 根据演化的曲率信号分布动态调整扰动预算，使稀疏零阶更新既保持聚焦性又具有足够探索性。

Result: 在OPT和Llama模型上的多种NLP任务实验表明，CurvZO相比零阶基线方法：1) 持续提高微调性能，准确率提升最高达4.4个百分点；2) 减少训练时间，实现最高2倍加速；3) 同时保持了内存效率。

Conclusion: CurvZO通过自适应曲率引导的稀疏零阶优化，有效解决了零阶优化中梯度估计方差大的问题，在保持内存效率的同时显著提升了LLM微调的性能和训练速度，为资源受限环境下的高效模型微调提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) with backpropagation achieves high performance but incurs substantial memory overhead, limiting scalability on resource-constrained hardware. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization provides a memory-efficient alternative by relying solely on forward passes, yet it typically suffers from slow or unstable convergence due to high-variance gradient estimates. Sparse ZO updates partially address this issue by perturbing only a subset of parameters, but their effectiveness hinges on selecting informative parameters, which is challenging in ZO optimization because each query yields only scalar feedback. We propose \textbf{Adaptive Curvature-Guided Sparse Zeroth-Order Optimization (CurvZO)}, which tracks curvature signals online from scalar ZO feedback and leverages these signals to construct a parameter-wise sampling distribution for selecting coordinates at each update, reducing the variance of the sparse ZO gradient estimator. Moreover, CurvZO dynamically adapts the perturbation budget to the evolving curvature signal distribution, yielding sparse ZO updates that remain both focused and sufficiently exploratory. Extensive experiments on OPT and Llama across diverse NLP tasks show that CurvZO consistently improves fine-tuning performance and reduces training time over ZO baselines. It improves accuracy by up to 4.4 points and achieves up to a $2\times$ speedup, while preserving memory efficiency.

</details>


### [166] [EvoIdeator: Evolving Scientific Ideas through Checklist-Grounded Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21728)
*Andreas Sauter,Yuyue Zhao,Jacopo Urbani,Wenxiang Hu,Zaiqiao Meng,Lun Zhou,Xiaohui Yan,Yougang Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoIdeator：基于清单反馈的强化学习框架，通过结合词典式奖励和细粒度语言反馈，促进科学思想的迭代进化，显著优于更大规模模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法依赖基于量表的标量奖励，缺乏可操作的细粒度指导；而基于语言的精炼方法通常局限于推理时提示，未针对内化此类批评进行优化。需要弥合这一差距，实现科学思想的高质量迭代进化。

Method: 提出EvoIdeator框架，将RL训练目标与清单反馈对齐。使用结构化评判模型生成两种协同信号：(1)用于多维优化的词典式奖励；(2)提供关于基础性、可行性和方法严谨性的细粒度语言反馈。将这些信号整合到RL循环中，使策略在优化和推理期间系统利用精确反馈。

Result: 基于Qwen3-4B构建的EvoIdeator在关键科学指标上显著优于更大的前沿模型。学习到的策略展现出对多样化外部反馈源的强泛化能力，无需进一步微调，为自主思想精炼提供了可扩展且严谨的路径。

Conclusion: EvoIdeator通过将RL训练与清单反馈对齐，有效促进了科学思想的迭代进化，为自主知识发现提供了可扩展且严谨的解决方案，展示了在科学思想生成任务上的优越性能。

Abstract: Scientific idea generation is a cornerstone of autonomous knowledge discovery, yet the iterative evolution required to transform initial concepts into high-quality research proposals remains a formidable challenge for Large Language Models (LLMs). Existing Reinforcement Learning (RL) paradigms often rely on rubric-based scalar rewards that provide global quality scores but lack actionable granularity. Conversely, language-based refinement methods are typically confined to inference-time prompting, targeting models that are not explicitly optimized to internalize such critiques. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{EvoIdeator}, a framework that facilitates the evolution of scientific ideas by aligning the RL training objective with \textbf{checklist-grounded feedback}. EvoIdeator leverages a structured judge model to generate two synergistic signals: (1) \emph{lexicographic rewards} for multi-dimensional optimization, and (2) \emph{fine-grained language feedback} that offers span-level critiques regarding grounding, feasibility, and methodological rigor. By integrating these signals into the RL loop, we condition the policy to systematically utilize precise feedback during both optimization and inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoIdeator, built on Qwen3-4B, significantly outperforms much larger frontier models across key scientific metrics. Crucially, the learned policy exhibits strong generalization to diverse external feedback sources without further fine-tuning, offering a scalable and rigorous path toward self-refining autonomous ideation.

</details>


### [167] [The Reasoning Error About Reasoning: Why Different Types of Reasoning Require Different Representational Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21736)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个表征系统结构特性的框架，识别了操作性、一致性、结构保持性和组合性四个特性，这些特性在不同推理形式中有不同需求，揭示了统计学习无法跨越的结构边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学、人工智能和心灵哲学领域缺乏对推理形式所需表征系统结构特性的系统性分析，需要建立一个统一的框架来理解不同推理类型对表征系统的结构需求。

Method: 提出一个理论框架，识别表征系统的四个结构特性：操作性、一致性、结构保持性和组合性，分析这些特性在不同推理形式（从归纳到演绎）中的需求程度，并推导可测试的预测。

Result: 框架揭示了主要的结构边界：边界以下的推理类型可以在关联性、概率性表征上操作，而边界以上的推理需要所有四个特性完全满足。统计学习的扩展无法跨越这一边界，因为演绎推理所需的结构保证无法通过概率手段近似实现。

Conclusion: 该框架是一个必要条件说明，对表征格式保持不可知论，旨在重组现有辩论而非终结它们。从人工智能评估、发展心理学和认知神经科学获得的趋同证据在不同直接性水平上支持该框架。

Abstract: Different types of reasoning impose different structural demands on representational systems, yet no systematic account of these demands exists across psychology, AI, and philosophy of mind. I propose a framework identifying four structural properties of representational systems: operability, consistency, structural preservation, and compositionality. These properties are demanded to different degrees by different forms of reasoning, from induction through analogy and causal inference to deduction and formal logic. Each property excludes a distinct class of reasoning failure. The analysis reveals a principal structural boundary: reasoning types below it can operate on associative, probabilistic representations, while those above it require all four properties to be fully satisfied. Scaling statistical learning without structural reorganization is insufficient to cross this boundary, because the structural guarantees required by deductive reasoning cannot be approximated through probabilistic means. Converging evidence from AI evaluation, developmental psychology, and cognitive neuroscience supports the framework at different levels of directness. Three testable predictions are derived, including compounding degradation, selective vulnerability to targeted structural disruption, and irreducibility under scaling. The framework is a necessary-condition account, agnostic about representational format, that aims to reorganize existing debates rather than close them.

</details>


### [168] [The Presupposition Problem in Representation Genesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21745)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出大型语言模型（LLMs）首次实现了高认知性能却未经历"表征生成"过程，这暴露了哲学心智理论在解释表征起源时的结构性缺陷——"表征预设"结构导致解释循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的出现使"表征生成"问题变得紧迫：如果LLMs没有经历从非表征物理系统到内容敏感行为指导系统的转变，哪些认知能力会受到影响？现有哲学框架无法回答这个问题，因为它们都预设了系统已经是表征者。

Method: 论文采用概念诊断而非提出新理论的方法，分析主要哲学框架（思维语言假说、目的语义学、预测处理、生成主义、遗传现象学）在解释表征生成时的共同结构缺陷——"表征预设"结构导致"表征回归"问题。

Result: 识别出"表征预设"这一系统性解释延迟模式：现有理论在解释表征起源时，都从表征过渡本身引入了概念资源，形成解释循环。这导致无法真正解释表征如何首次获得内容可操作的表征。

Conclusion: 论文建立了问题的结构框架，并推导出避免"表征预设"模式的任何理论必须满足的两个最低充分条件。LLMs使缺乏这种理论不再仅仅是理论问题，而是具有实际后果。

Abstract: Large language models are the first systems to achieve high cognitive performance without clearly undergoing representation genesis: the transition from a non-representing physical system to one whose states guide behavior in a content-sensitive way. Prior cognitive systems had already made this transition before we could examine it, and philosophy of mind treated genesis as a background condition rather than an explanatory target. LLMs provide a case that does not clearly involve this transition, making the genesis question newly urgent: if genesis did not occur, which cognitive capacities are affected, and why? We currently lack the conceptual resources to answer this. The reason, this paper argues, is structural. Major frameworks in philosophy of mind, including the Language of Thought hypothesis, teleosemantics, predictive processing, enactivism, and genetic phenomenology, share a common feature when applied to the genesis question: at some explanatory step, each deploys concepts whose explanatory purchase depends on the system already being organized as a representer. This pattern, which we call the Representation Presupposition structure, generates systematic explanatory deferral. Attempts to explain the first acquisition of content-manipulable representation within the existing categorical vocabulary import resources from the representational side of the transition itself. We call this the Representation Regress. The paper offers a conceptual diagnosis rather than a new theory, establishing the structure of the problem and deriving two minimum adequacy conditions for any account that avoids this pattern. LLMs make the absence of such a theory consequential rather than merely theoretical.

</details>


### [169] [Reasoning or Rhetoric? An Empirical Analysis of Moral Reasoning Explanations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21854)
*Aryan Kasat,Smriti Singh,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型在道德困境中表现出"道德腹语"现象：它们通过对齐训练习得了成熟道德推理的修辞惯例，但缺乏人类道德发展的实际轨迹，表现出道德解耦和跨困境一致性等非人类特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型是否真正具备道德推理能力，还是仅仅模仿道德推理的表面形式。研究旨在区分模型是否展现出真正的道德发展阶段进展（如科尔伯格理论），还是仅仅通过对齐训练产生了表面类似成熟道德判断的输出。

Method: 使用LLM-as-judge评分流程，通过三个法官模型验证，对13个不同架构、参数规模和训练机制的LLM在六个经典道德困境中的600多个回答进行分类。进行十项互补分析来表征所得模式的本质和内部一致性。

Result: 发现惊人的反转现象：无论模型大小、架构或提示策略如何，回答绝大多数对应于后习俗推理阶段（5-6阶段），这与人类发展规范（以第4阶段为主）相反。部分模型表现出道德解耦：陈述的道德理由与行动选择之间的系统性不一致，这种逻辑不连贯性在规模和提示策略中持续存在。模型规模有统计显著但实际影响小的效应；训练类型无显著独立主效应；模型表现出近乎机械的跨困境一致性。

Conclusion: 这些模式构成了"道德腹语"的证据：通过对齐训练习得了成熟道德推理的修辞惯例，但缺乏这些惯例所代表的底层发展轨迹。模型表现出道德解耦和逻辑一致性失败，表明其道德推理是表面的而非真实的。

Abstract: Do large language models reason morally, or do they merely sound like they do? We investigate whether LLM responses to moral dilemmas exhibit genuine developmental progression through Kohlberg's stages of moral development, or whether alignment training instead produces reasoning-like outputs that superficially resemble mature moral judgment without the underlying developmental trajectory. Using an LLM-as-judge scoring pipeline validated across three judge models, we classify more than 600 responses from 13 LLMs spanning a range of architectures, parameter scales, and training regimes across six classical moral dilemmas, and conduct ten complementary analyses to characterize the nature and internal coherence of the resulting patterns. Our results reveal a striking inversion: responses overwhelmingly correspond to post-conventional reasoning (Stages 5-6) regardless of model size, architecture, or prompting strategy, the effective inverse of human developmental norms, where Stage 4 dominates. Most strikingly, a subset of models exhibit moral decoupling: systematic inconsistency between stated moral justification and action choice, a form of logical incoherence that persists across scale and prompting strategy and represents a direct reasoning consistency failure independent of rhetorical sophistication. Model scale carries a statistically significant but practically small effect; training type has no significant independent main effect; and models exhibit near-robotic cross-dilemma consistency producing logically indistinguishable responses across semantically distinct moral problems. We posit that these patterns constitute evidence for moral ventriloquism: the acquisition, through alignment training, of the rhetorical conventions of mature moral reasoning without the underlying developmental trajectory those conventions are meant to represent.

</details>


### [170] [Tacit Knowledge Management with Generative AI: Proposal of the GenAI SECI Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21866)
*Naoshi Uchihira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"GenAI SECI"模型，作为知识创造过程(SECI)模型的更新版本，旨在利用生成式AI能力，通过引入"数字碎片化知识"概念，整合显性和隐性知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在变革知识管理，但现有研究主要关注显性知识管理，对于利用生成式AI整合显性和隐性知识的系统化建模仍不足。需要更新传统的SECI模型以适应生成式AI时代。

Method: 提出"GenAI SECI"模型，引入"数字碎片化知识"新概念，在赛博空间中整合显性和隐性知识。提供具体系统架构，并与具有相似问题意识和目标的前沿研究模型进行比较。

Result: 开发了更新的知识创造过程模型，通过生成式AI能力重新设计SECI模型，实现了显性和隐性知识的整合管理，提出了具体的系统实现方案。

Conclusion: "GenAI SECI"模型为生成式AI时代的知识管理提供了新的理论框架，通过数字碎片化知识概念和具体架构，解决了传统知识管理系统的局限性，推动了知识创造过程的现代化。

Abstract: The emergence of generative AI is bringing about a significant transformation in knowledge management. Generative AI has the potential to address the limitations of conventional knowledge management systems, and it is increasingly being deployed in real-world settings with promising results. Related research is also expanding rapidly. However, much of this work focuses on research and practice related to the management of explicit knowledge. While fragmentary efforts have been made regarding the management of tacit knowledge using generative AI, the modeling and systematization that handle both tacit and explicit knowledge in an integrated manner remain insufficient. In this paper, we propose the "GenAI SECI" model as an updated version of the knowledge creation process (SECI) model, redesigned to leverage the capabilities of generative AI. A defining feature of the "GenAI SECI" model is the introduction of "Digital Fragmented Knowledge", a new concept that integrates explicit and tacit knowledge within cyberspace. Furthermore, a concrete system architecture for the proposed model is presented, along with a comparison with prior research models that share a similar problem awareness and objectives.

</details>


### [171] [Guideline-grounded retrieval-augmented generation for ophthalmic clinical decision support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21925)
*Shuying Chen,Sen Cui,Zhong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Oph-Guid-RAG：用于眼科临床问答和决策支持的多模态视觉RAG系统，通过直接检索指南页面图像并设计可控检索框架，显著提升在困难临床问题上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决临床AI应用中证据基础薄弱的问题，特别是在需要精确、基于证据推理的挑战性眼科临床场景中。传统方法可能丢失指南中的表格、流程图和布局信息，且缺乏可控的检索机制来减少噪声。

Method: 将每个指南页面作为独立证据单元，直接检索页面图像以保留视觉信息。设计可控检索框架，包含路由和过滤机制，选择性引入外部证据。系统集成查询分解、查询重写、检索、重排序和多模态推理，并提供可追溯的指南页面引用输出。

Result: 在HealthBench的困难子集上，相比GPT-5.2，整体分数从0.2969提升至0.3861（+30.0%），准确率从0.5956提升至0.6576（+10.4%）。相比GPT-5.4，准确率增益更大（+24.4%）。消融研究表明重排序、路由和检索设计对稳定性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 结合基于视觉的检索与可控推理可以改善临床AI应用中的证据基础和鲁棒性。该方法在需要精确证据推理的挑战性案例中更有效，但需要进一步工作使其更完善。

Abstract: In this work, we propose Oph-Guid-RAG, a multimodal visual RAG system for ophthalmology clinical question answering and decision support. We treat each guideline page as an independent evidence unit and directly retrieve page images, preserving tables, flowcharts, and layout information. We further design a controllable retrieval framework with routing and filtering, which selectively introduces external evidence and reduces noise. The system integrates query decomposition, query rewriting, retrieval, reranking, and multimodal reasoning, and provides traceable outputs with guideline page references. We evaluate our method on HealthBench using a doctor-based scoring protocol. On the hard subset, our approach improves the overall score from 0.2969 to 0.3861 (+0.0892, +30.0%) compared to GPT-5.2, and achieves higher accuracy, improving from 0.5956 to 0.6576 (+0.0620, +10.4%). Compared to GPT-5.4, our method achieves a larger accuracy gain of +0.1289 (+24.4%). These results show that our method is more effective on challenging cases that require precise, evidence-based reasoning. Ablation studies further show that reranking, routing, and retrieval design are critical for stable performance, especially under difficult settings. Overall, we show how combining visionbased retrieval with controllable reasoning can improve evidence grounding and robustness in clinical AI applications,while pointing out that further work is needed to be more complete.

</details>


### [172] [Future-Interactions-Aware Trajectory Prediction via Braid Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22035)
*Caio Azevedo,Stefano Sabatini,Sascha Hornauer,Fabien Moutarde*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于辫子理论的辅助任务来提升多智能体轨迹预测性能，通过预测智能体间的交叉关系增强社会意识，在三个数据集上显著改进联合预测指标


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要准确预测多个交互智能体的未来行为，现有方法要么计算成本高，要么依赖启发式标注。辫子理论提供了一种精确描述多智能体行为的方式，但以往工作未充分利用其表达能力

Method: 提出辫子预测辅助任务，与轨迹预测并行执行。通过分类智能体间的交叉类型（辫子表示中的边），使模型获得改进的社会意识。该方法在训练和推理时仅增加可忽略的计算复杂度

Result: 在三个独立数据集上显著改进了联合预测指标。辫子预测任务使模型具备未来意图意识，产生更准确的联合预测

Conclusion: 充分利用辫子理论的表达能力并将其用于条件化轨迹预测，能显著提升多智能体轨迹预测性能。简单的辫子预测辅助任务能有效增强模型的社会意识，改善联合预测准确性

Abstract: To safely operate, an autonomous vehicle must know the future behavior of a potentially high number of interacting agents around it, a task often posed as multi-agent trajectory prediction. Many previous attempts to model social interactions and solve the joint prediction task either add extensive computational requirements or rely on heuristics to label multi-agent behavior types. Braid theory, in contrast, provides a powerful exact descriptor of multi-agent behavior by projecting future trajectories into braids that express how trajectories cross with each other over time; a braid then corresponds to a specific mode of coordination between the multiple agents in the future. In past work, braids have been used lightly to reason about interacting agents and restrict the attention window of predicted agents. We show that leveraging more fully the expressivity of the braid representation and using it to condition the trajectories themselves leads to even further gains in joint prediction performance, with negligible added complexity either in training or at inference time. We do so by proposing a novel auxiliary task, braid prediction, done in parallel with the trajectory prediction task. By classifying edges between agents into their correct crossing types in the braid representation, the braid prediction task is able to imbue the model with improved social awareness, which is reflected in joint predictions that more closely adhere to the actual multi-agent behavior. This simple auxiliary task allowed us to obtain significant improvements in joint metrics on three separate datasets. We show how the braid prediction task infuses the model with future intention awareness, leading to more accurate joint predictions. Code is available at github.com/caiocj1/traj-pred-braid-theory.

</details>


### [173] [SpecTM: Spectral Targeted Masking for Trustworthy Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22097)
*Syed Usama Imtiaz,Mitra Nasr Azadani,Nasrin Alamdari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SpecTM（光谱目标掩码），一种物理信息掩码设计，通过预训练中鼓励从跨光谱上下文重建目标波段，提升地球观测基础模型的可信度和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前地球观测基础模型通常依赖随机掩码，缺乏明确的物理约束，这在指导公共卫生决策的预测模型中构成关键可信度限制。需要开发能够编码光谱内在表示的物理信息学习方法。

Method: 提出SpecTM物理信息掩码设计，开发了可适应的多任务自监督学习框架，包括波段重建、生物光学指数推断和8天前时间预测三个任务，通过联合优化编码光谱内在表示。

Result: 在NASA PACE高光谱影像的微囊藻毒素浓度回归任务中，SpecTM达到R²=0.695（当前周）和R²=0.620（8天前预测），分别超过基线模型34%和99%。目标掩码比随机掩码提高0.037 R²，在极端稀缺条件下具有2.2倍标签效率优势。

Conclusion: SpecTM实现了跨地球观测领域的物理信息表示学习，提升了基础模型的可解释性，为物理约束的自监督学习提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Foundation models are now increasingly being developed for Earth observation (EO), yet they often rely on stochastic masking that do not explicitly enforce physics constraints; a critical trustworthiness limitation, in particular for predictive models that guide public health decisions. In this work, we propose SpecTM (Spectral Targeted Masking), a physics-informed masking design that encourages the reconstruction of targeted bands from cross-spectral context during pretraining. To achieve this, we developed an adaptable multi-task (band reconstruction, bio-optical index inference, and 8-day-ahead temporal prediction) self-supervised learning (SSL) framework that encodes spectrally intrinsic representations via joint optimization, and evaluated it on a downstream microcystin concentration regression model using NASA PACE hyperspectral imagery over Lake Erie. SpecTM achieves R^2 = 0.695 (current week) and R^2 = 0.620 (8-day-ahead) predictions surpassing all baseline models by (+34% (0.51 Ridge) and +99% (SVR 0.31)) respectively. Our ablation experiments show targeted masking improves predictions by +0.037 R^2 over random masking. Furthermore, it outperforms strong baselines with 2.2x superior label efficiency under extreme scarcity. SpecTM enables physics-informed representation learning across EO domains and improves the interpretability of foundation models.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Fusing Driver Perceived and Physical Risk for Safety Critical Scenario Screening in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20232)
*Chen Xiong,Ziwen Wang,Deqi Wang,Cheng Wang,Yiyang Chen,He Zhang,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于驾驶员风险融合的自动驾驶危险场景筛选方法，通过改进的驾驶员风险场和动态成本模型生成高质量风险监督信号，在推理时直接预测场景级风险分数，实现高效大规模排序和检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶测试从大规模自然驾驶数据中挖掘安全关键场景时，仍依赖人工风险标注和昂贵的逐帧风险评估，导致效率低下且风险量化基础薄弱。需要一种更高效、更准确的风险评估方法来支持大规模危险场景筛选。

Method: 1. 改进的驾驶员风险场：引入新的风险高度函数和速度自适应前瞻机制；2. 动态成本模型：整合动能、定向边界框约束和高斯核扩散平滑，实现更准确的交互建模；3. 风险轨迹交叉注意力解码器：联合解码风险和轨迹；4. 训练阶段结合改进风险场和动态成本模型生成高质量风险监督信号；5. 推理阶段通过前向传播直接预测场景级风险分数，避免逐帧计算。

Result: 在INTERACTION和FLUID数据集上实验表明，该方法产生更平滑、更具区分度的风险估计。在FLUID数据集上，AUC达到0.792，AP达到0.825，分别比PODAR方法提升9.1%和5.1%，证明了其在可扩展风险标注和危险场景筛选方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的驾驶员风险融合方法能够高效地从大规模自然驾驶数据中筛选危险场景，避免了昂贵的逐帧风险评估，为自动驾驶测试提供了可扩展的风险标注和场景筛选解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous driving testing increasingly relies on mining safety critical scenarios from large scale naturalistic driving data, yet existing screening pipelines still depend on manual risk annotation and expensive frame by frame risk evaluation, resulting in low efficiency and weakly grounded risk quantification. To address this issue, we propose a driver risk fusion based hazardous scenario screening method for autonomous driving. During training, the method combines an improved Driver Risk Field with a dynamic cost model to generate high quality risk supervision signals, while during inference it directly predicts scenario level risk scores through fast forward passes, avoiding per frame risk computation and enabling efficient large scale ranking and retrieval. The improved Driver Risk Field introduces a new risk height function and a speed adaptive look ahead mechanism, and the dynamic cost model integrates kinetic energy, oriented bounding box constraints, and Gaussian kernel diffusion smoothing for more accurate interaction modeling. We further design a risk trajectory cross attention decoder to jointly decode risk and trajectories. Experiments on the INTERACTION and FLUID datasets show that the proposed method produces smoother and more discriminative risk estimates. On FLUID, it achieves an AUC of 0.792 and an AP of 0.825, outperforming PODAR by 9.1 percent and 5.1 percent, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness for scalable risk labeling and hazardous scenario screening.

</details>


### [175] [EnergyAction: Unimanual to Bimanual Composition with Energy-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20236)
*Mingchen Song,Xiang Deng,Jie Wei,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnergyAction：通过能量基模型将单臂操作策略组合式迁移到双臂任务的新框架，无需大量双臂演示数据


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单臂操作策略在丰富数据和成熟架构下已取得显著成功，但扩展到双臂操作面临挑战：缺乏双臂演示数据、双臂动作协调复杂。现有方法要么依赖大量双臂数据集，要么未能有效利用预训练单臂策略。

Method: 1. 将单臂策略建模为能量基模型，利用其组合特性融合左右臂动作；2. 通过能量约束引入基于能量的时空协调机制；3. 提出两种能量感知去噪策略，根据动作质量评估动态调整去噪步骤。

Result: 实验结果表明，EnergyAction能有效将单臂知识迁移到双臂任务，在模拟和真实世界任务中均取得优异性能，且仅需少量双臂数据。

Conclusion: EnergyAction通过能量基模型的组合特性，实现了从单臂到双臂操作策略的有效迁移，解决了双臂操作中数据稀缺和协调复杂的问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in unimanual manipulation policies have achieved remarkable success across diverse robotic tasks through abundant training data and well-established model architectures. However, extending these capabilities to bimanual manipulation remains challenging due to the lack of bimanual demonstration data and the complexity of coordinating dual-arm actions. Existing approaches either rely on extensive bimanual datasets or fail to effectively leverage pre-trained unimanual policies. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{EnergyAction}, a novel framework that compositionally transfers unimanual manipulation policies to bimanual tasks through the Energy-Based Models (EBMs). Specifically, our method incorporates three key innovations. First, we model individual unimanual policies as EBMs and leverage their compositional properties to compose left and right arm actions, enabling the fusion of unimanual policies into a bimanual policy. Second, we introduce an energy-based temporal-spatial coordination mechanism through energy constraints, ensuring the generated bimanual actions are both temporal coherence and spatial feasibility. Third, we propose two different energy-aware denoising strategies that dynamically adapt denoising steps based on action quality assessment. These strategies ensure the generation of high-quality actions while maintaining superior computational efficiency compared to fixed-step denoising approaches. Experimental results demonstrate that EnergyAction effectively transfers unimanual knowledge to bimanual tasks, achieving superior performance on both simulated and real-world tasks with minimal bimanual data.

</details>


### [176] [Rheos: Modelling Continuous Motion Dynamics in Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20239)
*Iacopo Catalano,Francesco Verdoja,Javier Civera,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Julio A. Placed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rheos框架将连续方向运动模型嵌入3D场景图的动态层，使用半包裹高斯混合模型替代离散直方图，通过在线更新机制提升导航性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景图(3DSGs)对动态处理仅限于跟踪单个智能体，而动态地图(MoDs)依赖均匀网格离散化，缺乏语义基础且扩展性差。需要结合两者优势，在语义层次结构中嵌入连续动态模型

Method: 在分层3DSG中增加动态层，每个动态节点维护半包裹高斯混合模型捕获多模态方向流；采用储层采样进行有界内存观测缓冲，并行单元模型更新，以及基于贝叶斯信息准则(BIC)扫描选择最优混合成分数

Result: 在模拟行人环境中四个空间分辨率下评估，Rheos在连续和不利离散指标下始终优于离散基线；将每次更新初始化成本从样本数的二次方降低到线性

Conclusion: Rheos成功将连续动态模型嵌入3D场景图，提供原则性概率分布和显式不确定性建模，优于传统离散方法，并开源实现

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) provide hierarchical, multi-resolution abstractions that encode the geometric and semantic structure of an environment, yet their treatment of dynamics remains limited to tracking individual agents. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) complement this by modeling aggregate motion patterns, but rely on uniform grid discretizations that lack semantic grounding and scale poorly. We present Rheos, a framework that explicitly embeds continuous directional motion models into an additional dynamics layer of a hierarchical 3DSG that enhances the navigational properties of the graph. Each dynamics node maintains a semi-wrapped Gaussian mixture model that captures multimodal directional flow as a principled probability distribution with explicit uncertainty, replacing the discrete histograms used in prior work. To enable online operation, Rheos employs reservoir sampling for bounded-memory observation buffers, parallel per-cell model updates and a principled Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) sweep that selects the optimal number of mixture components, reducing per-update initialization cost from quadratic to linear in the number of samples. Evaluated across four spatial resolutions in a simulated pedestrian environment, Rheos consistently outperforms the discrete baseline under continuous as well as unfavorable discrete metrics. We release our implementation as open source.

</details>


### [177] [TRGS-SLAM: IMU-Aided Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Blurry, Rolling Shutter, and Noisy Thermal Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20443)
*Spencer Carmichael,Katherine A. Skinner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRGS-SLAM：基于3D高斯泼溅的热惯性SLAM系统，专门处理微测辐射热计热像仪的运动模糊、卷帘快门和固定模式噪声问题，在真实世界快速运动和高噪声热数据上实现精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热像仪在机器人SLAM中具有被动、低功耗、能在黑暗环境工作、对光照变化不敏感、能穿透雾尘等优势，但常用的非制冷微测辐射热计热像仪存在显著的运动模糊、卷帘快门失真和固定模式噪声，现有SLAM方法难以处理这些退化问题。

Method: 提出TRGS-SLAM系统，采用基于3D高斯泼溅的热惯性SLAM框架。创新包括：模型感知的3DGS渲染方法；B样条轨迹优化与两阶段IMU损失；基于视图多样性的不透明度重置；姿态漂移校正方案。

Result: 系统在真实世界快速运动和高噪声热数据上实现精确跟踪，而其他测试的SLAM方法均失败。通过离线精炼SLAM结果，实现了与需要真实姿态的先验工作相竞争的热图像恢复效果。

Conclusion: TRGS-SLAM是首个能够有效处理微测辐射热计热像仪特有退化问题的3DGS热惯性SLAM系统，在恶劣视觉条件下表现出鲁棒性能，为热视觉SLAM提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Thermal cameras offer several advantages for simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) with mobile robots: they provide a passive, low-power solution to operating in darkness, are invariant to rapidly changing or high dynamic range illumination, and can see through fog, dust, and smoke. However, uncooled microbolometer thermal cameras, the only practical option in most robotics applications, suffer from significant motion blur, rolling shutter distortions, and fixed pattern noise. In this paper, we present TRGS-SLAM, a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) based thermal inertial SLAM system uniquely capable of handling these degradations. To overcome the challenges of thermal data, we introduce a model-aware 3DGS rendering method and several general innovations to 3DGS SLAM, including B-spline trajectory optimization with a two-stage IMU loss, view-diversity-based opacity resetting, and pose drift correction schemes. Our system demonstrates accurate tracking on real-world, fast motion, and high-noise thermal data that causes all other tested SLAM methods to fail. Moreover, through offline refinement of our SLAM results, we demonstrate thermal image restoration competitive with prior work that required ground truth poses.

</details>


### [178] [High-Speed, All-Terrain Autonomy: Ensuring Safety at the Limits of Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20525)
*James R. Baxter,Bogdan I. Epureanu,Paramsothy Jayakumar,Tulga Ersal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型局部轨迹规划器，能够在崎岖地形上高速控制自主越野车辆，通过能量约束防止侧翻，实现实时计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主车辆在崎岖地形高速行驶时存在安全隐患，现有方法要么无法预测和防止地形引起的侧翻，要么无法满足实时性要求

Method: 开发了新的模型预测控制(MPC)公式，推导了非平面崎岖地形上的车辆动力学模型，采用基于能量的约束来安全支持极端机动（包括轮胎离地但不侧翻），通过并行GPGPU计算实现实时性

Result: 在模拟试验和全尺寸物理实验中验证了规划器的安全性，相比最先进的基线方法，在多个挑战性场景中实现了更少的侧翻和更高的成功率

Conclusion: 该规划器能够为自主越野车辆在崎岖地形上高速行驶提供安全、极端的轨迹规划，解决了现有方法在侧翻预防和实时性方面的不足

Abstract: A novel local trajectory planner, capable of controlling an autonomous off-road vehicle on rugged terrain at high-speed is presented. Autonomous vehicles are currently unable to safely operate off-road at high-speed, as current approaches either fail to predict and mitigate rollovers induced by rough terrain or are not real-time feasible. To address this challenge, a novel model predictive control (MPC) formulation is developed for local trajectory planning. A new dynamics model for off-road vehicles on rough, non-planar terrain is derived and used for prediction. Extreme mobility, including tire liftoff without rollover, is safely enabled through a new energy-based constraint. The formulation is analytically shown to mitigate rollover types ignored by many state-of-the-art methods, and real-time feasibility is achieved through parallelized GPGPU computation. The planner's ability to provide safe, extreme trajectories is studied through both simulated trials and full-scale physical experiments. The results demonstrate fewer rollovers and more successes compared to a state-of-the-art baseline across several challenging scenarios that push the vehicle to its mobility limits.

</details>


### [179] [Memory Over Maps: 3D Object Localization Without Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20530)
*Rui Zhou,Xander Yap,Jianwen Cao,Allison Lau,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需构建完整3D场景重建的轻量级目标定位方法，仅使用带姿态的RGB-D关键帧作为视觉记忆，通过检索、重排序和按需3D估计实现高效目标定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于3D重建的目标定位方法存在映射时间长、存储开销大和可扩展性限制的问题。本文探讨是否必须进行完整的3D场景重建才能实现目标定位，旨在开发更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出无地图流水线：仅存储带姿态的RGB-D关键帧作为轻量视觉记忆；查询时检索候选视图，用视觉语言模型重排序，通过深度反投影和多视图融合构建稀疏的按需3D目标估计。

Result: 相比基于重建的方法，预处理成本大幅降低，场景索引构建速度快两个数量级，存储需求显著减少。在多个基准测试中表现出色，验证了基于图像场景记忆的直接推理可有效替代密集3D重建。

Conclusion: 直接基于图像场景记忆进行推理可以有效替代密集3D重建，实现面向对象的机器人导航，为具身任务提供了更高效、轻量化的目标定位解决方案。

Abstract: Target localization is a prerequisite for embodied tasks such as navigation and manipulation. Conventional approaches rely on constructing explicit 3D scene representations to enable target localization, such as point clouds, voxel grids, or scene graphs. While effective, these pipelines incur substantial mapping time, storage overhead, and scalability limitations. Recent advances in vision-language models suggest that rich semantic reasoning can be performed directly on 2D observations, raising a fundamental question: is a complete 3D scene reconstruction necessary for object localization? In this work, we revisit object localization and propose a map-free pipeline that stores only posed RGB-D keyframes as a lightweight visual memory--without constructing any global 3D representation of the scene. At query time, our method retrieves candidate views, re-ranks them with a vision-language model, and constructs a sparse, on-demand 3D estimate of the queried target through depth backprojection and multi-view fusion. Compared to reconstruction-based pipelines, this design drastically reduces preprocessing cost, enabling scene indexing that is over two orders of magnitude faster to build while using substantially less storage. We further validate the localized targets on downstream object-goal navigation tasks. Despite requiring no task-specific training, our approach achieves strong performance across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating that direct reasoning over image-based scene memory can effectively replace dense 3D reconstruction for object-centric robot navigation. Project page: https://ruizhou-cn.github.io/memory-over-maps/

</details>


### [180] [LASER: Level-Based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime for Spatiotemporally Constrained Multi-Robot Timber Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20577)
*Zhenxiang Huang,Lior Skoury,Tim Stark,Aaron Wagner,Hans Jakob Wagner,Thomas Wortmann,Achim Menges*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LASER框架通过屏障机制和约束规划调度，实现多机器人系统在时空约束下的异步并行执行，应用于木材制造中的螺钉压胶工艺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 木材制造等大规模制造领域需要多机器人系统处理紧密耦合的时空约束（如碰撞避免和工艺驱动的截止时间），现有方法难以有效协调异步并行执行。

Method: 提出LASER框架：1) 将屏障机制集成到约束规划调度中，将任务划分为时空不相交的层级；2) 机器人可在层级内并行异步执行，仅在层级屏障处同步；3) 针对大规模问题提出两种专用算法：异构任务序列的迭代时间松弛方法和同质任务的双层分解方法。

Result: 成功制造了2.4m×6m全尺寸木材板，使用双机器人系统协调108个子程序和352个螺钉，在紧密的粘合剂时间窗口内完成；计算研究表明该方法相比整体方法具有稳定的扩展性。

Conclusion: LASER框架通过层级化屏障机制实现了多机器人系统的安全异步并行执行，解决了大规模制造中的时空约束协调问题，在木材制造应用中验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Automating large-scale manufacturing in domains like timber construction requires multi-robot systems to manage tightly coupled spatiotemporal constraints, such as collision avoidance and process-driven deadlines. This paper introduces LASER (Level-based Asynchronous Scheduling and Execution Regime), a complete framework for scheduling and executing complex assembly tasks, demonstrated on a screw-press gluing application for timber slab manufacturing. Our central contribution is to integrate a barrier-based mechanism into a constraint programming (CP) scheduling formulation that partitions tasks into spatiotemporally disjoint sets, which we define as levels. This structure enables robots to execute tasks in parallel and asynchronously within a level, synchronizing only at level barriers, which guarantees collision-free operation by construction and provides robustness to timing uncertainties. To solve this formulation for large problems, we propose two specialized algorithms: an iterative temporal-relaxation approach for heterogeneous task sequences and a bi-level decomposition for homogeneous tasks that balances workload. We validate the LASER framework by fabricating a full-scale 2.4m x 6m timber slab with a two-robot system mounted on parallel linear tracks, successfully coordinating 108 subroutines and 352 screws under tight adhesive time windows. Computational studies show our method scales steadily with size compared to a monolithic approach.

</details>


### [181] [Unified Orbit-Attitude Estimation and Sensor Tasking Framework for Autonomous Cislunar Space Domain Awareness Using Multiplicative Unscented Kalman Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20579)
*Smriti Nandan Paul,Siwei Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出先进的地月空间态势感知框架，包含观测架构优化和传感器任务调度优化，通过数值仿真验证了在减少传感器数量的同时获得更优性能，并揭示了目标-观测器比例对姿态估计的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间存在强非线性、非开普勒动力学特性，导致不确定性传播和状态估计精度下降。此外面临长距离观测需求、受限的传感器-目标几何关系与光照条件、广阔监测区域以及传感器布局设计空间大等挑战，需要开发先进的地月空间态势感知方法。

Method: 提出包含两个关键任务的框架：(1) 基于真实成本函数的观测架构优化，使用Tree of Parzen Estimators算法求解；(2) 在离散任务调度间隔进行互信息驱动的传感器任务优化，同时在连续任务更新之间使用误差状态乘法无迹卡尔曼滤波器进行轨道和姿态状态估计。

Result: 任务1的观测架构优化相比基线随机搜索方案显著降低了成本函数值且使用更少传感器。任务2显示平移状态估计在各种目标-观测器比例下均表现良好，而姿态估计对目标-观测器比例和任务调度间隔更为敏感，在高目标数量和低频任务更新时出现旋转状态发散。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了传感资源、任务调度频率和可达到的状态估计性能之间的重要权衡，这些因素影响自主地月空间态势感知的可扩展性。姿态估计对目标-观测器比例和任务调度间隔的敏感性表明需要针对不同应用场景优化资源配置。

Abstract: The cislunar regime departs from near-Earth orbital behavior through strongly non-linear, non-Keplerian dynamics, which adversely affect the accuracy of uncertainty propagation and state estimation. Additional challenges arise from long-range observation requirements, restrictive sensor-target geometry and illumination conditions, the need to monitor an expansive cislunar volume, and the large design space associated with space/ground-based sensor placement. In response to these challenges, this work introduces an advanced framework for cislunar space domain awareness (SDA) encompassing two key tasks: (1) observer architecture optimization based on a realistic cost formulation that captures key performance trade-offs, solved using the Tree of Parzen Estimators algorithm, and (2) leveraging the resulting observer architecture, a mutual information-driven sensor tasking optimization is performed at discrete tasking intervals, while orbital and attitude state estimation is carried out at a finer temporal resolution between successive tasking updates using an error-state multiplicative unscented Kalman filter. Numerical simulations demonstrate that our approach in Task 1 yields observer architectures that achieve significantly lower values of the proposed cost function than baseline random-search solutions, while using fewer sensors. Task 2 results show that translational state estimation remains satisfactory over a wide range of target-to-observer count ratios, whereas attitude estimation is significantly more sensitive to target-to-observer ratios and tasking intervals, with increased rotational-state divergence observed for high target counts and infrequent tasking updates. These results highlight important trade-offs between sensing resources, tasking cadence, and achievable state estimation performance that influence the scalability of autonomous cislunar SDA.

</details>


### [182] [Towards Practical World Model-based Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20607)
*Zhilong Zhang,Haoxiang Ren,Yihao Sun,Yifei Sheng,Haonan Wang,Haoxin Lin,Zhichao Wu,Pierre-Luc Bacon,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-MBPO：一种基于模型的世界模型框架，通过统一多模态模型、交错视图解码和分块分支展开来解决VLA模型在机器人控制中强化学习微调的实际挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人控制中展现出强大的泛化能力，但使用强化学习进行微调面临真实世界交互的高成本和安全性风险。在交互式世界模型中训练VLA模型可以避免这些问题，但引入了像素级世界建模、多视图一致性和稀疏奖励下的误差累积等挑战

Method: VLA-MBPO框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 采用统一多模态模型进行数据高效的世界建模；(2) 交错视图解码机制来强制多视图一致性；(3) 分块级分支展开策略来缓解误差累积问题

Result: 理论分析和仿真及真实世界任务的实验表明，VLA-MBPO显著提高了策略性能和样本效率，证明了其在真实世界机器人部署中的鲁棒性和可扩展性

Conclusion: VLA-MBPO为解决VLA模型在机器人控制中强化学习微调的挑战提供了一个实用框架，通过创新的世界建模和训练策略，实现了高效、安全的策略优化，为真实世界机器人部署提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong generalization for robotic control, but finetuning them with reinforcement learning (RL) is constrained by the high cost and safety risks of real-world interaction. Training VLA models in interactive world models avoids these issues but introduces several challenges, including pixel-level world modeling, multi-view consistency, and compounding errors under sparse rewards. Building on recent advances across large multimodal models and model-based RL, we propose VLA-MBPO, a practical framework to tackle these problems in VLA finetuning. Our approach has three key design choices: (i) adapting unified multimodal models (UMMs) for data-efficient world modeling; (ii) an interleaved view decoding mechanism to enforce multi-view consistency; and (iii) chunk-level branched rollout to mitigate error compounding. Theoretical analysis and experiments across simulation and real-world tasks demonstrate that VLA-MBPO significantly improves policy performance and sample efficiency, underscoring its robustness and scalability for real-world robotic deployment.

</details>


### [183] [Speedup Patch: Learning a Plug-and-Play Policy to Accelerate Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20658)
*Zhichao Wu,Junyin Ye,Zhilong Zhang,Yihao Sun,Haoxin Lin,Jiaheng Luo,Haoxiang Ren,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SuP是一个轻量级、策略无关的框架，通过离线数据实现即插即用的执行加速，使用外部调度器自适应下采样动作块来消除冗余，在保持任务成功率的同时实现1.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身策略虽然展现出卓越的操作技能，但执行速度过慢，因为它们继承了人类演示的缓慢节奏。现有的加速方法通常需要策略重新训练或昂贵的在线交互，限制了大规模基础模型的可扩展性。

Method: 提出Speedup Patch (SuP)框架，引入外部调度器自适应下采样具身策略提供的动作块以消除冗余。将调度器优化形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程(CMDP)，旨在最大化效率而不损害任务性能。由于离线设置中无法直接评估成功率，SuP引入基于世界模型的状态偏差作为替代指标来强制执行安全约束。通过使用学习的世界模型作为虚拟评估器来预测反事实轨迹，调度器可以通过离线强化学习进行优化。

Result: 在仿真基准测试(Libero, Bigym)和真实世界任务上的实证结果表明，SuP为多样化策略实现了整体1.8倍的执行加速，同时保持了原始的成功率。

Conclusion: SuP提供了一个轻量级、策略无关的解决方案，能够在不重新训练策略或进行在线交互的情况下，仅使用离线数据实现具身策略的即插即用加速，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: While current embodied policies exhibit remarkable manipulation skills, their execution remains unsatisfactorily slow as they inherit the tardy pacing of human demonstrations. Existing acceleration methods typically require policy retraining or costly online interactions, limiting their scalability for large-scale foundation models. In this paper, we propose Speedup Patch (SuP), a lightweight, policy-agnostic framework that enables plug-and-play acceleration using solely offline data. SuP introduces an external scheduler that adaptively downsamples action chunks provided by embodied policies to eliminate redundancies. Specifically, we formalize the optimization of our scheduler as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) aimed at maximizing efficiency without compromising task performance. Since direct success evaluation is infeasible in offline settings, SuP introduces World Model based state deviation as a surrogate metric to enforce safety constraints. By leveraging a learned world model as a virtual evaluator to predict counterfactual trajectories, the scheduler can be optimized via offline reinforcement learning. Empirical results on simulation benchmarks (Libero, Bigym) and real-world tasks validate that SuP achieves an overall 1.8x execution speedup for diverse policies while maintaining their original success rates.

</details>


### [184] [E-SocialNav: Efficient Socially Compliant Navigation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20664)
*Ling Xiao,Daeun Song,Xuesu Xiao,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出E-SocialNav，一个用于机器人社会合规导航的高效语言模型，在保持导航成功率的同時强调社会合规性，并通过两阶段训练在小型数据集上超越零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人导航基准主要关注导航成功率而忽视社会合规性，同时大规模语言模型存在计算开销大、响应慢、能耗高等效率问题，不适用于资源受限的实时机器人平台部署。

Method: 提出E-SocialNav高效语言模型，采用两阶段训练流程：先进行监督微调，再进行直接偏好优化，在相对较小的数据集上训练，专门针对社会合规导航任务。

Result: E-SocialNav在生成社会合规行为方面持续优于零样本基线，在文本级语义相似度和动作准确性方面均表现强劲，尽管训练数据集相对较小。

Conclusion: 通过专门设计的训练方法和架构，可以在保持社会合规性的同时实现高效的机器人导航，为资源受限的机器人平台提供实用的语言模型解决方案。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to robotic navigation; however, existing benchmarks primarily emphasize navigation success rates while paying limited attention to social compliance. Moreover, relying on large-scale LMs can raise efficiency concerns, as their heavy computational overhead leads to slower response times and higher energy consumption, making them impractical for real-time deployment on resource-constrained robotic platforms. In this work, we evaluate the social compliance of GPT-4o and Claude in robotic navigation and propose E-SocialNav, an efficient LM designed for socially compliant navigation. Despite being trained on a relatively small dataset, E-SocialNav consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines in generating socially compliant behaviors. By employing a two-stage training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization, E-SocialNav achieves strong performance in both text-level semantic similarity to human annotations and action accuracy. The source code is available at https://github.com/Dr-LingXiao/ESocialNav.

</details>


### [185] [ROI-Driven Foveated Attention for Unified Egocentric Representations in Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20668)
*Xinhai Sun,Xiang Shi,Menglin Zou,Wenlong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于感兴趣区域(ROI)的工程化工作流，通过前向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到外部相机，生成运动对齐的手部中心ROI，实现跨机器人数据重用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身AI系统受限于物理交互数据的可用性和结构，现有VLA模型存在数据收集成本高、跨具身对齐有限、从互联网视觉数据到机器人控制迁移效果差等问题。

Method: 提出ROI驱动的工程化工作流，引入以自我为中心、几何基础的数据表示。通过前向运动学将末端执行器姿态投影到单个外部相机，生成运动对齐的手部中心ROI，无需腕部相机或多视角系统。ROI从原始图像裁剪后调整大小，保留接触关键区域的高局部信息密度和全局上下文。

Result: 开发了可复现的流水线，涵盖校准、同步、ROI生成、确定性边界处理和元数据管理。生成的表示具有具身对齐和视角归一化特性，支持异构机器人间的数据重用。

Conclusion: 以自我为中心的ROI作为实用的数据抽象，支持可扩展的数据收集和跨具身学习，桥接互联网规模的感知和机器人特定的控制。

Abstract: The development of embodied AI systems is increasingly constrained by the availability and structure of physical interaction data. Despite recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models, current pipelines suffer from high data collection cost, limited cross-embodiment alignment, and poor transfer from internet-scale visual data to robot control.
  We propose a region-of-interest (ROI) driven engineering workflow that introduces an egocentric, geometry-grounded data representation. By projecting end-effector poses via forward kinematics (FK) into a single external camera, we derive movement-aligned hand-centric ROIs without requiring wrist-mounted cameras or multi-view systems. Unlike directly downsampling the full frame, ROI is cropped from the original image before resizing, preserving high local information density for contact-critical regions while retaining global context.
  We present a reproducible pipeline covering calibration, synchronization, ROI generation, deterministic boundary handling, and metadata governance. The resulting representation is embodiment-aligned and viewpoint-normalized, enabling data reuse across heterogeneous robots. We argue that egocentric ROI serves as a practical data abstraction for scalable collection and cross-embodiment learning, bridging internet-scale perception and robot-specific control.

</details>


### [186] [Enhancing Vision-Based Policies with Omni-View and Cross-Modality Knowledge Distillation for Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.20679)
*Kai Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过知识蒸馏将全视角深度策略的知识迁移到轻量级单目策略，解决移动机器人视觉策略在场景迁移性、计算资源和传感器成本方面的三难问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人上的视觉策略面临场景迁移性有限、板载计算资源受限和传感器硬件成本高的三难问题，需要一种既能保持高性能又轻量化的解决方案

Method: 提出知识蒸馏方法，不仅让学生策略模仿专家动作，还使其与全视角深度教师策略的潜在嵌入对齐，将信息丰富、外观不变的全视角深度策略知识迁移到轻量级单目策略

Result: 实验表明全视角和深度输入提升了场景迁移和导航性能，提出的蒸馏方法相比仅模仿动作的策略，显著提高了单目单视角策略的性能，真实世界实验进一步验证了方法的有效性和实用性

Conclusion: 通过知识蒸馏将全视角深度策略的知识迁移到轻量级单目策略，有效解决了移动机器人视觉策略的三难问题，实现了高性能与轻量化的平衡，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Vision-based policies are widely applied in robotics for tasks such as manipulation and locomotion. On lightweight mobile robots, however, they face a trilemma of limited scene transferability, restricted onboard computation resources, and sensor hardware cost.
